Model before rewriting:

find b: set (maxSize 2) of int(1..2)

such that

(a subset b)

new variables:
  find a#explicit_1: matrix indexed by [int(1..2)] of int(1..2)

new constraints:
  or([a#explicit_2 < 2,([SafeIndex(a#explicit_1,[1]);int(1..)] <lex [SafeIndex(a#explicit_1,[2]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  or([1 <= a#explicit_2,SafeIndex(a#explicit_1,[1]) = 1;int(1..)])
  or([2 <= a#explicit_2,SafeIndex(a#explicit_1,[2]) = 1;int(1..)])
